## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

#### Pattern 1: Affects 24 page(s) - 25 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R11: Link elements have an accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r11)

```html
<a href="tel:<a href="tel:19786827099">978-682-7099</a>" />
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/a
```

## Detailed Failure Information (ALFA)

### https://www.masshiremvwb.org/

#### Rule: [SIA-R11: Link elements have an accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r11)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.4.4](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/link-purpose-in-context), [SC 2.4.9](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/), [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="tel:<a href="tel:19786827099">978-682-7099</a>" />`
- XPath: `/a`

#### Rule: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.5.5](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a href="https://www.masshiremvwb.org/wp-content/uploads/FY27-BUDGET-FORMS-FOR-PROCUREMENT-COL-Youth-AI.xlsx">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<button class="sbi_load_btn" type="button">...</button>`
- XPath: `/button[@class="sbi_load_btn"]`

#### Rule: [SIA-R53: Headings do not skip heading levels in the document outline](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r53)
**WCAG**: Best Practice

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: The heading skips one or more levels
- HTML: `<h3 style="text-align: center; --fontSize: 28; line-height: 1.2;" data-fontsize="28" data-lineheight="33.6px" class="fusion-responsive-typography-calculated">...</h3>`
- XPath: `/h3[@class="fusion-responsive-typography-calculated"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: The heading skips one or more levels
- HTML: `<h4 class="entry-title fusion-responsive-typography-calculated" data-fontsize="24" style="--fontSize: 24; line-height: 1.2; --minFontSize: 24;" data-lineheight="28.8px">...</h4>`
- XPath: `/h4[@class="entry-title fusion-responsive-typography-calculated"]`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: The heading skips one or more levels
- HTML: `<h4 class="widget-title fusion-responsive-typography-calculated" data-fontsize="18" style="--fontSize: 18; line-height: 1; --minFontSize: 18;" data-lineheight="18px">Connect</h4>`
- XPath: `/h4[@class="widget-title fusion-responsive-typography-calculated"]`

#### Rule: [SIA-R56: Landmarks with the same role are distinguishable by their accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r56)
**WCAG**: Best Practice

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: Some `contentinfo` have the same name.

#### Rule: [SIA-R57: Visible text content on the page is contained within a landmark region](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r57)
**WCAG**: Best Practice

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: The text is not included in a landmark region

#### Rule: [SIA-R62: Links within blocks of text are visually distinguishable from surrounding text](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r62)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.1](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/use-of-color) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: The link is not distinguishable from the surrounding text
- HTML: `<a href="https://www.masshiremvwb.org/eolwd-secretary-lauren-jones-visit/"> [...]</a>`
- XPath: `/a`

#### Rule: [SIA-R66: Text has enhanced contrast with its background](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r66)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.6](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-enhanced) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-04
- Message: The highest possible contrast of the text is 3.65:1 which is         below the required contrast of 4.5:1

#### Rule: [SIA-R67](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r67)

**Failure 1:**
